# Errors on PO files


## Error with polib
We use the po metadata to get the language code and the team, but sometimes it fails, here are the files.

| Package | Lang from file path/name | polib error | Team |
|---------|--------------------------|-------------|------|
 | [bleachbit]({{< ref "/f41/package/bleachbit.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f41/bleachbit/po/ru.po | error-os |  |

 | [vrms-rpm]({{< ref "/f41/package/vrms-rpm.md" >}}) | /mnt/stockage/packages/f41/vrms-rpm/lang/ru.po | error-os |  |
